# 18 Product-intent crosswalk (late coverage check)

Supporting artifact. The product-knowledge corpus (`._docs/product-knowledge/`, 1,885 `WD-IDEA-*` ideas) was used ONLY as a search checklist after the code model was built. Every idea id is assigned to exactly one concept cluster below with the relation the CODE has to it. `WD-IDEA-*` ids are product intent and never evidence; `WD-IMPL-*` ids are code evidence. Legacy `impl` tags were ignored. Relation vocabulary: EXACT_MATCH, PARTIAL_MATCH, NOT_IMPLEMENTED (searched, nothing found), CONTRADICTED_BY_CODE, UNKNOWN (static analysis cannot settle).

## Totals (ideas)

| relation | ideas |
|---|---|
| CONTRADICTED_BY_CODE | 83 |
| EXACT_MATCH | 269 |
| NOT_IMPLEMENTED | 517 |
| PARTIAL_MATCH | 915 |
| UNKNOWN | 101 |

Clusters: 635. Coverage reports with missed-concept lists: `_evidence/reports/PK1-coverage.md` to `PK7-coverage.md`.
